# Node.js compatibility

Generated by `node scripts/compat.mjs` on 2026-10-24 at baa645c (do not edit by hand). Also on the web: <https://harryzz.github.io/js2wasi3/>.

Every API in node v24.21.0's documentation ([all.json](https://nodejs.org/docs/v24.21.0/api/all.json), 3044 rows) checked against js2wasi3: a probe program
tests each name under node and under js2wasi3 on wasmtime 50.0.0; stubs that throw are found in js2wasi3's sources; a row is
test-backed when a fixture that `scripts/diff.sh` checks against node names it. Presence isn't conformance: a present API
can still differ from node in details. Language conformance is the test262 section below.

| Status | Rows | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| supported | 706 | present in js2wasi3 and named in a passing fixture (each fixture is checked against node) |
| present | 310 | present in js2wasi3; no fixture names it yet |
| partial | 16 | present, with a known gap (see the note) |
| refused | 96 | present, but throws when called: the facility doesn't exist under WASI or isn't built yet |
| missing | 564 | node has it, js2wasi3 doesn't (or rejects it at build time) |
| no module | 298 | the module isn't one js2wasi3 ships: importing it is a build error |
| not probed | 1051 | events, instance-only properties, and names not reachable from a module or global |
| n/a | 3 | not an API of a running program |
